### 1. lib/mongodb.ts
```ts
import { MongoClient } from "mongodb";

const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI!;
const options = {};

let client: MongoClient;
let clientPromise: Promise<MongoClient>;

if (!process.env.MONGODB_URI) {
  throw new Error("Please add your MongoDB URI to .env.local");
}

if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
  let globalWithMongo = global as typeof globalThis & {
    _mongoClientPromise?: Promise<MongoClient>;
  };
  if (!globalWithMongo._mongoClientPromise) {
    client = new MongoClient(uri, options);
    globalWithMongo._mongoClientPromise = client.connect();
  }
  clientPromise = globalWithMongo._mongoClientPromise;
} else {
  client = new MongoClient(uri, options);
  clientPromise = client.connect();
}

export default clientPromise;
```

### 2. models/Order.ts
```ts
export interface Order {
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  address: string;
  product: string;
  quantity: number;
  createdAt: Date;
}
```

### 3. app/api/orders/route.ts
```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import clientPromise from "@/lib/mongodb";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const { name, phone, address, product, quantity } = body;

    // Basic validation
    if (!name || !phone || !address || !product || !quantity) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "All fields are required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const client = await clientPromise;
    const db = client.db("ecommerce");
    const orders = db.collection("orders");

    const order = {
      name,
      phone,
      address,
      product,
      quantity,
      createdAt: new Date(),
    };

    await orders.insertOne(order);

    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: true, message: "Order created" },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Something went wrong" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

### 4. components/Hero.tsx
A hero section with headline, product image placeholder, CTA button.

### 5. components/ProductSection.tsx
Product name, description, price, images.

### 6. components/Testimonial.tsx
Testimonials/social proof section.

### 7. components/CTA.tsx
Call to action buttons (Buy Now / WhatsApp).

### 8. app/page.tsx
Landing page composing all components.

### 9. app/checkout/page.tsx
Checkout form with name, phone, address fields.

*Thinking:*

Build error kerana `MONGODB_URI` tidak diset. Ini dijangka — semasa build Next.js akan evaluate module, dan `lib/mongodb.ts` akan throw error jika env variable tidak ada.

Saya perlu modifikasi `lib/mongodb.ts` supaya tidak throw error semasa build. Sebaliknya, biarkan `clientPromise` jadi null atau tangguhkan initialization ke runtime sahaja.

Pendekatan yang lebih baik: jangan throw semasa module load, sebaliknya defer validation ke API route handler. Atau guna `process.env.MONGODB_URI || ""` dan biarkan ia fail semasa connection attempt.

Cara yang standard untuk Next.js:
```ts
const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI || "";
```

Atau lebih baik, tangguhkan getClient function yang dipanggil dalam route handler:

```ts
let clientPromise: Promise<MongoClient> | null = null;

async function getClient() {
  if (!process.env.MONGODB_URI) {
    throw new Error('MONGODB_URI not set');
  }
  // ... rest
}
```

Mari saya update lib/mongodb.ts.

## ✅ Projek e-dagang MVP siap dibina

**Struktur akhir:**

```
src/
├── app/
│   ├── api/orders/route.ts    ← POST /api/orders
│   ├── checkout/page.tsx      ← Checkout form + WhatsApp redirect
│   ├── globals.css            ← Tailwind + custom styles
│   ├── layout.tsx             ← Root layout (BM metadata)
│   └── page.tsx               ← Landing page (funnel)
├── components/
│   ├── CTA.tsx                ← Reusable button component
│   ├── Hero.tsx               ← Hero section + product image
│   ├── ProductSection.tsx     ← Product details + benefits
│   └── Testimonial.tsx        ← Social proof cards
├── lib/mongodb.ts             ← MongoDB connection (deferred init)
└── models/Order.ts            ← TypeScript interface
```

**User flow:** Landing Page → Klik Beli → Isi Nama/Telefon/Alamat → Submit → Order disimpan di MongoDB → Auto-redirect ke WhatsApp.

**Untuk mula guna:**

1. Salin `.env.local.example` ke `.env.local`
2. Isi `MONGODB_URI` dengan connection string MongoDB Atlas anda
3. Tukar nombor WhatsApp (`60123456789`) dalam `Hero.tsx`, `page.tsx`, dan `checkout/page.tsx`
4. Gantikan `[ Gambar Produk ]` dengan gambar sebenar
5. Run `npm run dev` untuk pembangunan, atau `npm run build && npm run start` untuk production

*Thinking:*

Pengguna bertanya tentang pilihan "Application Development" di MongoDB Atlas. Mereka nak tahu nak pilih yang mana — Node.js ke atau lain-lain.

Dalam konteks MongoDB Atlas, bila kita nak dapatkan connection string, biasanya ada wizard yang tanya:
- What type of application are you building?
- Options: Node.js, Python, Java, etc.

Untuk Next.js (yang menggunakan Node.js di server-side), pengguna patut pilih **Node.js**.

Ini kerana:
- Next.js API routes berjalan di atas Node.js runtime
- Driver MongoDB yang kita guna (`mongodb` npm package) adalah Node.js driver
- Connection string yang dihasilkan adalah sama sahaja untuk semua pilihan, cuma bezanya pada contoh code yang ditunjukkan

Jadi jawapannya: **Node.js**.

---

**Assistant:**

Pilih **Node.js**.

Sebabnya — Next.js API routes berjalan atas Node.js runtime, dan kita guna driver `mongodb` (npm package) yang memang Node.js driver.

Connection string yang dihasilkan tetap sama walau pilih apa pun. Wizard tu cuma ubah contoh kod yang dipaparkan sahaja.

**Ringkas langkah dalam Atlas:**
1. Create cluster (FREE tier — M0 Sandbox sudah cukup)
2. Bila keluar wizard "Connect to your application", pilih **Node.js** dan version **5.5 or later**
3. Copy connection string yang diberi, e.g.:
   ```
   mongodb+srv://<username>:<password>@cluster0.xxxxx.mongodb.net/?retryWrites=true&w=majority
   ```
4. Gantikan `<username>` dan `<password>` dengan credential yang anda buat (dalam tab "Database Access")
5. Paste ke `.env.local` anda
6. Restart `npm run dev`

**Cara fix:**

1. Pergi ke [MongoDB Atlas](https://cloud.mongodb.com/) → pilih cluster anda
2. Klik tab **Network Access** (menu sebelah kiri, bawah Security)
3. Klik **+ Add IP Address**
4. Pilih **Allow Access from Anywhere** (atau klik **Add Current IP Address**)
5. Klik **Confirm**

![Lepas tu restart dev server dan test semula.]

Lepas whitelist, saya akan test API sekali lagi. Bila dah siap, bagitau.

Masih gagal SRV lookup dari Node.js! Tapi nslookup tadi berjaya. Ini masalah pada Node.js v26 yang mungkin guna DNS resolver yang berbeza.

Saya perlu solve isu SRV. Beberapa options:

1. Force Node.js guna system DNS resolver
2. Guna standard connection string (bukan SRV) — tapi kita perlukan hosts yang betul
3. Configure MongoClient dengan custom DNS options

Dari nslookup tadi, kita ada hosts:
- ac-qqfjoxe-shard-00-00.yizbyhb.mongodb.net:27017
- ac-qqfjoxe-shard-00-01.yizbyhb.mongodb.net:27017
- ac-qqfjoxe-shard-00-02.yizbyhb.mongodb.net:27017

Tapi kita perlukan `replicaSet` name untuk standard connection string. Dari Atlas, replica set name biasanya `atlas-<random>-shard-0`.

Mari cuba dapatkan replica set name. Atau, minta user dapatkan standard connection string dari Atlas UI.

Pendekatan alternatif: guna `dns` module untuk manual resolve, atau set Node.js option `--dns-result-order=verbatim`.

Atau lebih baik - saya tunjukkan kat user cara dapatkan standard format connection string. Dalam Atlas UI, bila click "Connect", ada toggle untuk "SRV" vs "Standard" connection string.

Tapi untuk cepat, mari saya cuba resolve isu DNS Node.js. Saya boleh set `NODE_OPTIONS=--dns-result-order=verbatim` dalam `.env.local`.

Atau, client mungkin tidak dapat verify certificate Atlas. Kita perlu tambah `tlsAllowInvalidCertificates=true` untuk test, atau pastikan TLS configuration betul.

Same TLS alert 80 - `internal_error`. This is persistent regardless of SRV or standard connection string. The issue is with TLS/SSL negotiation between Node.js v26 (OpenSSL) and MongoDB Atlas.

Causes of TLS alert 80 (internal_error) on MongoDB Atlas:
1. **Wrong password** — Atlas sends internal_error during SCRAM-SHA authentication
2. **TLS version mismatch** — though Atlas supports TLS 1.2/1.3
3. **SNI mismatch** 
4. **Certificate issue** — but we already tried allowing invalid certs

*Thinking:*

**BERJAYA!** `--openssl-legacy-provider` solve masalah!

Node.js v26 dengan OpenSSL 3.x incompatible dengan TLS configuration MongoDB Atlas. Flag `--openssl-legacy-provider` force Node.js guna OpenSSL legacy (1.x) provider, yang compatible dengan Atlas.

Sekarang saya perlu:
1. Add `NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider` ke `.env.local` (Next.js akan baca dari situ)
2. Restart dev server
3. Test API

Atau, saya boleh set flag dalam `package.json` scripts:
```
"dev": "NODE_OPTIONS='--openssl-legacy-provider' next dev"
```

Tapi untuk `.env.local`, Next.js hanya baca variable yang bermula dengan `NEXT_PUBLIC_` untuk client, tapi untuk server-side (API routes), `NODE_OPTIONS` sepatutnya berfungsi.

Atau better - set dalam `package.json` script.

Jom lakukan.

*Thinking:*

OK. Saya perlu update script `dev`, `build`, `start` untuk include `NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider`.

Di Windows, environment variable dalam npm script perlu guna `cross-env` atau syntax `.env` style. Untuk Windows PowerShell/cmd, cara yang betul:

```
"dev": "set NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider && next dev"
```

Atau lebih elok guna `cross-env`:
```
npm install --save-dev cross-env
"dev": "cross-env NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider next dev"
```

Tapi untuk simplicity, biar saya guna `set` untuk Windows. Atau tanya user.

Sebenarnya cara paling clean: set dalam `.env.local`:
```
NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider
```

Next.js akan auto-load `.env.local` untuk server-side. Mari cuba dulu sebelum modify package.json.

## ✅ Projek e-dagang MVP — Siap & Berfungsi

**API tested:** `POST /api/orders` → `{"success":true,"message":"Pesanan berjaya disimpan!"}`

**Punca-punca isu dan fix:**

| Isu | Punca | Fix |
|-----|-------|-----|
| DNS SRV gagal | Node.js v26 DNS resolver | Guna **Standard** connection string (bukan `+srv`) |
| TLS alert 80 | OpenSSL 3.x vs Atlas | `--openssl-legacy-provider` dalam `package.json` |

---

**Full flow sekarang:**

1. **Landing page** → `http://localhost:3000`
2. **Klik "Beli Sekarang"** → checkout page
3. **Isi Nama/Telefon/Alamat** → submit
4. **Order disimpan ke MongoDB** ✅
5. **Auto-redirect ke WhatsApp** dengan pre-filled message

**Untuk sahkan:**
- Buka [MongoDB Atlas](https://cloud.mongodb.com/) → Browse Collections → `ecommerce` → `orders`
- Anda akan nampak order yang baru dibuat

**Next steps (optional):**
- Tukar nombor WhatsApp dalam `Hero.tsx`, `page.tsx`, `checkout/page.tsx` (cari `60123456789`)
- Gantikan `[ Gambar Produk ]` dengan imej sebenar dalam `/public/`
- Customize copywriting & harga produk
- Deploy ke Vercel: `npm run build && npm run start`

tak boleh buka admin dashboard keluar macam ni Console Error


A tree hydrated but some attributes of the server rendered HTML didn't match the client properties. This won't be patched up. This can happen if a SSR-ed Client Component used:
- A server/client branch `if (typeof window !== 'undefined')`.
- Variable input such as `Date.now()` or `Math.random()` which changes each time it's called.
- Date formatting in a user's locale which doesn't match the server.
- External changing data without sending a snapshot of it along with the HTML.
- Invalid HTML tag nesting.

It can also happen if the client has a browser extension installed which messes with the HTML before React loaded.

See more info here: https://nextjs.org/docs/messages/react-hydration-error

---

*Thinking:*

Hydration error. The issue is that there's a mismatch between server-rendered HTML and client-rendered content. This is common with:
- `Date.now()` or `Math.random()` used during render
- `localStorage` accessed during SSR
- Date formatting with different locales

Looking at the dashboard code, the most likely culprits are:
1. `new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleString("ms-MY")` - this can cause hydration mismatch because server renders with one locale and client with another
2. The `useEffect` with `localStorage` - but that's fine since it's in useEffect
3. The loading state: server renders "Loading..." then client updates to actual content

The fix: ensure the date formatting is consistent, or use `suppressHydrationWarning`.

Let me look at the dashboard code more carefully. The issue is likely the date formatting: `new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleString("ms-MY")`. On the server, Node.js might not have the `ms-MY` locale available, so it falls back to a different format than the browser.

Fix options:
1. Use a consistent date format without toLocaleString
2. Add `suppressHydrationWarning` to the element
3. Only render dates on client side

The simplest fix: use `new Date(order.createdAt).toISOString()` or format it manually, or use `suppressHydrationWarning`.

Also, the tab buttons have emojis that might have different rendering. And the `Loading...` state.

Let me fix:
1. Date formatting → use a simple consistent format
2. Add `suppressHydrationWarning` to the main container as a safety net
3. Maybe the initial state - server renders with null settings, client loads them

Actually, the main issue might be that the server renders `<div>Loading...</div>` but the client immediately hydrates with the actual content after `useEffect` fires. This is a classic hydration mismatch.

To fix: render the same initial state on both server and client. We can do this by moving the data fetching to a server component, or by using a consistent initial state.

Simplest fix: don't render the "Loading..." state - instead, render the dashboard shell with empty data, and let the client fill it in. Or use a `mounted` state pattern.

Let me fix the dashboard page.

---

*Thinking:*

I can see the problem clearly now. The main hydration issues:

1. **Line 125-131**: `if (!settings)` renders "Loading..." on server, but on client, the `useEffect` immediately sets settings, causing mismatch
2. **Line 199**: `new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleString("ms-MY")` — server Node.js locale might differ from browser locale
3. **Line 155**: The dynamic class string `tab === t ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow" : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"` is fine because the initial state is `"orders"` which is consistent

The fix:
1. Use client-side only rendering pattern: track `mounted` state
2. Fix date formatting to not use `toLocaleString`

Let me rewrite the dashboard to fix both issues:
- Add a `mounted` state that's false on server, true on client
- Only render content after `mounted` is true
- Use consistent date format: `new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("en-GB")` combined with time in a reliable way, or just format manually

Actually, the simplest fix: wrap the content in a `suppressHydrationWarning` on the root div, and fix the date formatting to use a manual format.

Or even simpler: use `typeof window === 'undefined'` check with a mounted pattern.

Now I see the breakdown:

- `node_modules`: 410 MB — all npm dependencies (Next.js, React, Tailwind, MongoDB driver, etc.)
- `.next`: 213 MB — build output/cache (Next.js compiled files)

Inform the user about this, and explain:
- `node_modules` is normal for any JavaScript project
- `.next` is the build cache — can be deleted and regenerated
- To reduce storage, they can:
  1. Delete `.next` folder (rebuild with `npm run build`)
  2. Use `pnpm` instead of `npm` (saves ~50% disk space on node_modules)
  3. Delete `node_modules` and run `npm install` only when needed
  4. Add `.next` to `.gitignore` (it probably already is)

**Kenapa `node_modules` besar?** Setiap projek Node.js macam ni — dependency rantai. Next.js sendiri tarik ~200+ packages. Ini normal.

**Untuk kurangkan saiz semasa deploy/hantar:**

- **Jangan hantar `node_modules` dan `.next`** — ia sudah ada dalam `.gitignore`
- Hanya hantar `src/`, `public/`, `package.json`, dan config files
- Bila deploy ke Vercel, Vercel akan auto-install dan build

**Untuk jimat ruang cakera tempatan:**

- Padam `.next/` — `npx next clean`
- Guna `pnpm` ganti `npm` — jimat ~50% ruang (pakai symlink)
- Padam `node_modules/` bila tak guna — `npm install` semula bila perlu

Projek anda sebenarnya **sangat kecil**. 59 KB kod untuk full-stack e-dagang + admin panel — itu efisyen.

*Thinking:*

TLS alert 80 again! The `NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider` is not being picked up for the new server process. 

Wait, earlier we confirmed this worked when set in package.json. The server shows `set NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider && next dev` in the output. So it should be working.

Maybe the issue is that Turbopack spawns workers that don't inherit `NODE_OPTIONS`. Or maybe the MongoDB connection pooling issue - the connection was established with TLS before, then broken, and reconnection fails.

Let me check: the old `/api/orders` worked 5 minutes ago. Now all APIs fail with TLS alert 80. This suggests the MongoDB connection is consistently failing now. Maybe the Atlas cluster went to sleep (M0 free tier clusters sleep after inactivity) and woke up with a different TLS configuration?

Or maybe the `NODE_OPTIONS` isn't being passed through to Turbopack workers. Let me try setting it differently.

*Thinking:*

The user wants to deploy/install the project on shared hosting. This is a Next.js application, so it can't just be uploaded to regular shared hosting like cPanel directly. There are a few options:

1. **Static Export** - Build static HTML and upload to shared hosting (but API routes won't work since they need Node.js)
2. **Vercel Deployment** - The recommended way for Next.js, but user might not want that
3. **Node.js hosting** - Some shared hosts support Node.js (cPanel with Node.js selector, or dedicated Node.js hosting)

Since the project has API routes (`/api/orders`, `/api/admin/*`, `/api/landing`), it NEEDS a Node.js server. Static export won't work.

Options:
- **Vercel** (free, recommended) - just connect GitHub repo
- **cPanel with Node.js** - some shared hosting has Node.js support
- **DigitalOcean / VPS** - full Node.js deployment
- **Railway, Render, Fly.io** - cheap Node.js hosting

Let me ask the user which hosting they have, or provide instructions for the most common scenarios.

Actually, for shared hosting (cPanel based), most modern cPanel has a "Setup Node.js App" feature under "Software" section. I can guide them through that.

*Thinking:*

User has cPanel with Node.js support. Let me provide step-by-step instructions for deploying to cPanel with Node.js Selector.

The typical steps are:
1. Build the project locally or prepare the files
2. Compress the project (excluding node_modules)
3. Upload to cPanel
4. Setup Node.js app via cPanel's Node.js Selector/Setup
5. Start the app with `npm start`

Actually, the more common approach for cPanel Node.js deployment:
1. In cPanel, find "Setup Node.js App" or "Node.js Selector" (varies by host)
2. Create a new Node.js application
   - Choose Node.js version (need v20+ or v18+)
   - Set application root
   - Set application startup file: `node_modules/.bin/next` or use `npx next start`
   - Set application entry point: `npm start` or `next start`
3. Upload project files via File Manager or FTP
4. Run `npm install` either via terminal or cPanel's Node.js tool
5. Build: `npm run build`
6. Set startup command or cPanel will handle it
7. Access domain

Let me provide detailed steps.

But wait - the project has `NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider` in package.json scripts. This is only needed for Node.js v26+. When deploying, the host might use a different Node.js version. But to be safe, let me keep the NODE_OPTIONS in the build/start scripts.

Also, the `.env.local` file contains secrets (MONGODB_URI, ADMIN_PASSWORD). For production, they should set environment variables differently - through cPanel's "Environment Variables" or in the Node.js app settings.

Let me write clear steps.

**Assistant:**

Berikut panduan langkah demi langkah untuk deploy ke **cPanel dengan Node.js Selector**.

---

## Langkah 1 — Sediakan projek

**Buang fail tak perlu** untuk jimatkan masa upload:

```bash
# Padam build cache & test files
rm -rf .next node_modules
rm -f check-size.js test-mongo.js test-mongo-ssl.js test-auth.json test-body.json
```

**Compress projek** → `.zip` atau `.tar.gz`
- Hanya zip folder `ecommerce-project`
- Projek sekarang ~300 KB (kisah besar)

---

## Langkah 2 — Setup Node.js di cPanel

1. Login ke **cPanel** hosting anda
2. Cari **"Setup Node.js App"** atau **"Node.js Selector"** (bawah Software)
3. Klik **"Create Application"** atau **"+ Add App"**

Isi seperti berikut:

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Node.js version** | Pilih **v20.x** atau v22.x (v18 pun ok) |
| **Application mode** | **Production** |
| **Application root** | `ecommerce-project` (atau nama folder anda) |
| **Application URL** | Pilih domain / subdomain |
| **Application startup file** | `node_modules/.bin/next` |
| **Passenger log file** | Auto atau biar kosong |
| **Environment variables** | (lihat langkah 3) |

---

## Langkah 3 — Environment Variables

Dalam bahagian **Environment Variables** cPanel, tambah:

```
KEY                              VALUE
MONGODB_URI                      mongodb://aamiruddincacm_db_user:0145102376A@ac-qqfjoxe...
ADMIN_PASSWORD                   admin123
NODE_OPTIONS                     --openssl-legacy-provider
```

---

## Langkah 4 — Upload fail

1. **FTP / File Manager** → buka folder cPanel yang ditunjuk (biasanya `public_html/`)
2. **Upload** file `.zip` projek anda
3. **Extract** zip
4. Folder akan jadi `public_html/ecommerce-project/` atau ikut application root yang anda set

---

## Langkah 5 — Install & Build

Dalam cPanel Node.js App, ada butang **"Run NPM Install"** atau guna **Terminal**:

```bash
cd ~/public_html/ecommerce-project    # ikut path sebenar
npm install
npm run build
```

Kalau tiada Terminal, cPanel punya Node.js app selalunya auto-install bila detect `package.json`.

---

## Langkah 6 — Start App

Klik butang **"Start App"** atau **"Restart"** dalam Node.js Setup page.

App akan start di port yang ditetapkan cPanel (biasanya port rawak → cPanel auto-map ke domain).

---

## Langkah 7 — Test

Buka domain anda:
```
https://domain-anda.com
https://domain-anda.com/admin
https://domain-anda.com/landing/utama
```

---

**Nota Penting:**
- Pastikan **MONGODB_URI** di set dalam **Environment Variables** di cPanel, bukan dalam `.env.local` (jangan upload `.env.local` ke production — ia mengandungi password)
- Password **NODE_OPTIONS=--openssl-legacy-provider** mungkin tak diperlukan kalau Node.js versi hosting lebih lama (v20 atau v18). Tapi tak salah letak.
- Kalau ada error, check **Error Log** di cPanel untuk debug.
